1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the bonding structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 985. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 238 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to bonding theory, we decompose the problem into 985 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 238. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Theorem 219.4 (Invariance Principle)
